- SW install hotfix (unblocks push in production): the service worker never activated on the live site — `STATIC_ASSETS` used root-absolute paths (`/index.html`, …) that 404 under the `/Azfit.ai/` subpath, so `cache.addAll` rejected and install aborted. `public/sw.js` now precaches scope-relative paths (`./`, `./index.html`, `./manifest.json` + logos), precache is failure-tolerant (criticals as a group with `.catch()`, optional logos via individual `cache.add().catch()` so one missing asset can never kill install again), caches bumped to `azfit-v2`/`azfit-api-v2` to cleanly replace the dead v1 state. DEVIATION from brief (same root-cause class, not a strategy change): the offline navigate fallback `caches.match('/index.html')` had the identical root-absolute bug (it could never match the scope-relative precache entry) — fixed to `./index.html`; fetch strategy, push/notificationclick handlers, and registerSW.ts untouched. Verified dist/sw.js has only scope-relative precache paths (fix/sw-install)

Verified: gates green; browser — Profile Details pencil → modal opens with Alex's data prefilled → cancel; Eunice (0 programs) → empty state → Build Program lands at `/ai-program-builder?clientId=2a025db8…` with "Tailored to Eunice Tang's intake profile"; Alex (2 active) → cards shown, no empty state (feat/overview-polish)
- Schedule day-actions (Phase 27D): the ClientProfile Schedule day panel is now a full action menu — Book a session (unchanged 26D path), Add holiday, Add reminder, Request measurements, Request photos (the last three share one reminder dialog with preset titles "Measure weight + body fat" / "Progress photos due" / custom). Holiday saves ONE `sessions` row (type='holiday' — free-text type column, no migration) spanning start 00:00 → end 23:59 local AND flips `clients.status='on_holiday'` with the "set them Active when they're back" toast (manual revert via the client-list inline dropdown — no auto-revert in v1). Reminders save `sessions` rows (type='reminder', 30 min at 09:00 local). Calendar + panel render both types with icons (Sun/amber holiday, Bell/cyan reminder); `getEventsForDay` now matches any event whose [start,end] range COVERS the day, so multi-day holidays show on every covered day (cross-month verified). Account-less clients keep the "Scheduling activates…" note covering ALL actions. ScheduleTab also takes an optional `clientsId` prop for the status flip. Verified: gates green; REST — holiday INSERT spanning 3 days → 201, clients.status PATCH → 200 on_holiday, both read back, restored active + deleted; smoke (Playwright fallback — the owner's browser was closed mid-phase, WebBridge had no window) — signed in, all 5 actions present, holiday Jul 30→Aug 1 saved + status flipped to on_holiday, Aug 1 panel shows Holiday (multi-day coverage), reminder "Check sleep quality" in panel, reload persists (Jul 30 cell shows both dots), SQL confirmed rows + cleanup (feat/schedule-day-actions)
- Meal plans (Phase 27E): new `meal_plans` table (one active plan per client in v1 — overwrite after confirm; items as jsonb; targets snapshot jsonb) keyed on clients(id) with RLS copied from client_goals (works for account-less clients). `src/lib/mealPlan.ts` — pure deterministic generator: restriction/allergy term filtering (name/brand/category, case-insensitive), vegetarian/vegan keyword filters (documented short lists), 30/30/30/10 calorie split, 2–4 items per meal (protein-density preferred for mains), servings scaled to the slice and rounded to 5g (15g floor), seeded PRNG so Regenerate shuffles; 12 unit tests. `MealPlanCard` on the client Nutrition tab (BOTH profile modes — uses the tab's normalized targets + intake restrictions/diet): empty state → Generate (foods from `foods_cache`), per-meal sections with per-item Swap, daily totals vs targets, draft/saved chips, Save (INSERT first time, UPDATE after a window.confirm overwrite), Regenerate. Verified: gates green (54 tests incl. 12 new); migration proof (table + RLS + 2 policies matching client_goals); REST — trainer INSERT → 201, client SELECT own → visible, client INSERT for another client_id → 403, cleanup → 0 rows; smoke (Playwright) — generated against a temporary "chicken" restriction on Alex, saved, regenerated (plan changed), persisted after reload; SQL proof 0 chicken items in the saved plan's 12 items; test rows + restriction restored (feat/meal-plans)
- Photo compare + status dropdown fix (Phase 27F): FIXED the 27A inline status dropdown rendering transparent/under rows — root cause was stacking (later transformed rows painted above the panel) not the background: the open row now gets its own stacking context (`position: relative; z-index: 30`), the panel is solid `var(--card-bg)` + border + strong shadow, `z-50`, `max-h-80` with `overflow-y-auto` (all 11 statuses + descriptions reachable), closes on outside click AND Escape (new global keydown listener); the Other-filter menu got the same treatment. PhotoGallery's basic compare upgraded to the full design: numbered selection badges 1/2 (insertion-ordered Set), "Pick two photos to compare — same category works best" hint, a "View comparison" button when 2 are selected opening a full-screen `ComparisonModal` (side-by-side on desktop, stacked at 375px, caption bars with category/date/weight/BF, "before/after" tags, and a delta line like "-3.5 kg · 24 days" when both have weights; close returns to compare-mode selection). Milestones, lightbox, upload, filters untouched. Verified: gates green; smoke A (Playwright) — panel opaque rgb(255,255,255), z-50, overflowY auto, 11 options, Escape closes, outside click closes; smoke B — seeded 2 test photos, compare hint, 2/2 selection with numbered badges, modal renders captions + "-3.5 kg · 24 days" delta, desktop 2-col / mobile stacked, close returns to selection, deselect works, normal lightbox works after exit; test photos deleted from storage + metadata (feat/photo-compare)
- Status dropdown clipping hotfix: the 27A/27F menu was still clipped by the table card's overflow boundary on the LAST row — it now renders in a body PORTAL (`createPortal`) with `position: fixed`, anchored to the clicked badge's `getBoundingClientRect()`: viewport-aware flip (opens upward when space below < 340px and above is larger, downward otherwise — both directions smoke-verified), horizontal clamped to the viewport edges (8px margin), repositions on scroll/resize while open, max-height 320px with scroll. The Other-filter menu shares the same `PortalMenu` component. IMPLEMENTATION BUG caught by smoke: reading `e.currentTarget` inside the setState updater threw (React nullifies currentTarget after dispatch — the menu silently never opened); rect is now captured BEFORE the updater. Close behavior unchanged: outside click + Escape; 27F's opacity/shadow/scroll intact. Verified: gates green; Playwright — last row opens upward fully inside viewport with all 11 options, first row opens downward at 900px viewport (upward at 800px per the spec formula), narrow viewport no horizontal overflow, Escape + outside click close, status selection still updates (fix/status-dropdown-clipping)
- Client invite flow (Phase 28A): trainers can now share an invite link that auto-links signups. New public route `/invite/:trainerId` (`src/pages/Invite.tsx`) — "Train with {name}" via `get_trainer_display_name(uuid)` SECURITY DEFINER rpc (full_name only, granted to anon — profiles RLS untouched), friendly invalid-link state, buttons into `/signup?trainer=` and `/login?trainer=`. `src/lib/inviteLink.ts` persists the param in sessionStorage and, after successful auth, creates the client's row (`status: 'pending_start'`) via the new "Invited clients can create own record" INSERT policy (email must equal the caller's auth email through a second SECURITY DEFINER helper `current_user_email()` — DEVIATION from the brief's inline `auth.users` subquery: the `authenticated` role has no SELECT privilege on `auth.users` and the policy failed with 42501 until the helper replaced it). Existing row for another trainer → untouched + toast; same trainer → no duplicate. Clients page header has an "Invite link" button copying the live `#/invite/<trainer id>` URL. Verified: gates green; migration proof (policy + both functions live, anon rpc returns name / null for unknown); E2E (Playwright) — invite page anon shows "Train with Coach Demo (DEMO)", invalid link state, signup lands with `?trainer=`, SQL-created test user signs in via `?trainer=` → clients row auto-created `pending_start` with the right trainer_id + "You're connected with Coach Demo" toast visible, second login no duplicate, trainer's Invite link button copies the correct URL; test user fully cleaned up. ENV NOTE: fresh-account signup via the form is currently blocked by Supabase's email-send rate limit (429 `over_email_send_rate_limit`) — the signup-with-session link path is identical code and activates once the quota resets (feat/invite-flow)
- Schedule page picker + mark completed (Phase 28B): FIXED the main schedule page's booking dialog receiving `clients={[]}` — Schedule.tsx now fetches the trainer's clients and resolves each to a `profiles.id` via email (sessions.client_id references profiles, so account-less clients are omitted with a comment noting why) and passes the mapped array to BookSessionDialog — the picker lists real clients again and booking from /schedule lands the correct client_id. Session interaction additions (trainer-only, in EditSessionDialog): "Mark completed" for scheduled/requested sessions in the past (`updateSession(id, { status: 'completed' })`) and "Accept" for requested sessions (`status: 'scheduled'`); `CalendarEvent` gained an optional `status` field (sessionToEvent maps it), and completed sessions render muted (`bg-slate-700/80`, 70% opacity) with an emerald check badge on the calendar so history reads differently from upcoming. Verified: gates green; REST — past test session created → 201, PATCH status=completed → 200, read-back confirmed, deleted; smoke (Playwright) — context menu → Book Client → picker lists "Alex Carter", booked today's 06:00 session onto the calendar, clicked it → Mark completed → muted card with check badge → reload persists (verified in SQL + muted card render); smoke row deleted (fix/schedule-picker-complete)
- Trainer dashboard widgets (Phase 28C): "Today at a Glance" replaces the plain Today's Sessions section — sessions (unchanged item style) mixed by time with Holiday items (amber, sun icon — sessions type='holiday' covering today), Reminders (cyan, bell — type='reminder' today, incl. the 27D measurement/photo presets), and Returning (green — clients with status='on_holiday' whose latest holiday session ends today or within 7 days); empty day shows "Nothing scheduled today — enjoy the calm." with an Open Full Calendar link; collapse/expand kept. New `FollowUpsWidget` right after the client-health grid: three capped groups computed client-side from real queries — No session in 5+ days (per-client last non-cancelled session, "never" for none), BioPrint overdue 30+ (latest skinfold assessment or none + client 30+ days old → "Enter BioPrint" to `/client/<id>?tab=bio`), No active program (active clients with zero active programs → "Build" to `/ai-program-builder?clientId=`); red count pill hidden at 0, renders nothing when zero, "View all" → /clients. DEVIATIONS from the source doc (as noted in the brief): the "Urgent / payment overdue" item type is DROPPED (no paymentStatus exists anywhere in this codebase); "Returning" is computed from sessions type='holiday' ends_at + clients.status='on_holiday' (no holidayEnd column exists); BioPrint actions link to the client's Bio History tab (`?tab=bio` — /bioprint takes no clientId). Verified: gates green; live data check as trainer — 0 sessions/holidays/reminders today (calm path), follow-up computation from real rows flags Ben/Jonny/sdsds/Eunice (never), Alex (8d), nobody on BioPrint or no-program correctly; smoke (Playwright) — Today at a Glance title + calm message + footer link, Follow-Ups renders with "last session 8d ago" + "no sessions yet" rows + red pill, Build action lands at `/ai-program-builder?clientId=` (feat/dashboard-widgets)
- Wizard safety engine (Phase 28D): contraindication flags + guided swaps in the AI Program Builder. New data module `src/data/exerciseSafety.ts` — 8 doc-verbatim contraindication rows (lower back + cardiovascular = EXCLUDE severity; knee/shoulder/wrist/neck/hip/pregnancy = warn), wizard LIMITATIONS→vocabulary alias map, findContraindications/safetyNoteFor helpers. New pure lib `src/lib/programSafety.ts` — collectClientLimitations merges wizard chips + otherLimitation free text + intake injuries (keyword normalization; unmatched free text → generic warn on compounds only), evaluateProgramSafety flags per exercise (exclude pulls top-3 alternatives via findExerciseSubstitutions, warn pulls none); 15 unit tests incl. alias mapping, severity levels, free-text, empty passthrough. Generator pass: `applySafetyFilter` replaces filterByInjuries in aiProgramGenerator — exclude-severity pool entries are SWAPPED for their top DB substitution (dropped when no candidate), warn entries stay for trainer review; deterministic, pure, no-op without injuries. Step 6 (Exercise Review) UI: memoized flags per day list — SafetyBanner (red tint when any exclude, amber warn-only, "N exercises conflict with client limitations"), flagged rows get a red/amber left border + ShieldAlert icon, and the row expander shows limitation + doc note + up to 3 [Swap → name] buttons + [Keep Original]; Swap preserves sets/reps/tempo/rest, marks isSubstituted + safetyNote, toasts "Swapped for safety"; Keep Original records "Trainer override: kept despite <limitation>". Persistence: exerciseNotes/parseExerciseNotes JSON extended additively with optional isSubstituted + safetyNote (old rows parse unchanged — verified on saved rows), Step7Preview + Step8Save show an amber "N safety substitutions" chip. Verified: gates green (69 unit tests incl. 15 safety); smoke (Playwright) — auto-generate for Alex with intake injuries=knee → warn banner on Exercise Review, wizard Lower-back chip → exclude banner, added Deadlift row flagged "Lower back pain" → Swap → BB Deadlift - Blocks with sets preserved 3→3 + toast, Back Squat Keep Original, chips on Preview + Save steps, Save & Assign persisted — SQL read-back shows isSubstituted:true + safetyNote on the swapped row and the Trainer-override note on the kept row (SMOKE28D program + intake injuries cleaned up afterward). SMOKE NOTE: auto-generate lands on step index 6 (Program Preview), and it resets assignedClient + clientContext.limitations (pre-existing behavior) — the smoke re-selects the client on the Save step (feat/wizard-safety)
- Nutrition engine (Phase 28E): percentage goal adjustments + safety guardrails + lean-mass macros in `src/lib/tdee.ts` (Features 1-3 of the PHASE6_NUTRITION doc; carb cycling / command center / adherence are later phases). ADDITIVE: legacy `GOAL_ADJUSTMENTS`, `calculateGoalCalories`, `calculateMacroBreakdown` stay exported with @deprecated JSDoc pointing at the new functions (all 7 legacy tests keep passing untouched). STEP 1 — `GOAL_ADJUSTMENTS_PCT` (−20/−10/0/+5/+10 incl. NEW lean_gain), `MAX_KCAL_DELTA` = 1000, `calculateGoalCaloriesPct` (pct delta clamped to ±1000 kcal, never below 0; code comment documents the Part A cap vs Part D raw-percentage spec ambiguity — Part A cap implemented). STEP 2 — `applySafetyGuardrails(target, bmr, tdee)` → { calories, clamped, warnings[] }: floor BMR×1.2, ceiling TDEE+1000, warns never silently; replaces Math.max(0) as the pipeline's final clamp. STEP 3 — `calculateMacroTargets` implementing spec D6 exactly: protein on lean mass (LBM = weight×(1−BF%) or ×0.8 unknown; 2.0 base / 2.2 for BF>25% or gain/aggressive goals / 1.8 for BF<12% or vegetarian-vegan / hard cap 1.6 with kidneyConcern; also capped at 35% of kcal), fats at 25% of remaining kcal with 0.6 g/kg and female 25%-of-kcal floors, carbs fill remainder, fiber 14 g/1000 kcal floored 25(f)/38(m), water 35 ml/kg + 500 ml/training day rounded to 50 ml. TdeeCalculator: goal selector iterates the 5 pct goals (Lean Gain (+5%) between Maintenance and Muscle Gain) with a computed-delta helper line ("+245 kcal/day", "capped at ±1,000" when clamped), amber guardrail banner ("Target raised to BMR × 1.2 safety floor: N kcal"), result panel gains Fiber + Water rows; the SAVED target shape is unchanged (calories/protein/carbs/fats only — fiber/water are display-only). Verified: gates green; 88 unit tests (19 new — pct clamp incl. 6000-TDEE cap case, floor/ceiling warnings, full multiplier ladder incl. kidney cap + 35% cap, fat floors, fiber minimums, water rounding); smoke (Playwright, Alex Nutrition tab) — 5 goal options render in spec order, Lean Gain helper text, Sedentary+Aggressive forces the amber floor banner, Fiber/Water rows present, banner clears at Maintenance, Apply toasts and SQL read-back shows the same 4-column shape with the new algorithm's values (2740/128/418/62, macro-kcal ≈ target); Alex's original targets restored afterward (feat/nutrition-engine)
- Per-day exercise lists fix (Phase 29A): generated programs no longer clone one exercise list across all days. ROOT CAUSE was NOT the generator — generateProgram() already builds distinct per-day workouts (per-day slotCategories + a shared usedExercises dedupe set) and is untouched; the bug was `mapGeneratedToProgramData` in AIProgramBuilder.tsx, which mapped only activeWorkouts[0] into shared `data.exercises` and never set `data.workoutExercises`, so the save path wrote the same list to every day. FIX (surgical): the mapper now builds `workoutExercises` — each generated workout's own exercises mapped with the same field mapping (order→code, sets, reps, PCT_MAP pct1RM, tempo, rest s→m:ss) under key idx+1 (1-based day index; packed split.days land Mon=1…Thu=4 exactly as the existing split UI logic already assumed) — and keeps `data.exercises` as a COPY-mirror of the first non-empty day (same convention as rehydrateProgramData) for shared-list consumers. This automatically switches Step 6 into per-day mode and makes the save path write distinct rows. No changes to the generator, save mapper, rehydrate path, Step 6 rendering, or schema. New unit test `src/pages/AIProgramBuilder.test.ts` (exported the mapper with a one-line react-refresh disable, matching repo precedent): 6 tests — 4-day Upper/Lower yields keys 1-4, each list equal to its own generated workout's names, all 4 lists distinct, mirror convention, split-day alignment, field mapping. Verified: gates green (94 tests); smoke (Playwright) — 4-day generate for Alex → Exercise Review day chips Mon·Upper A / Tue·Lower A / Wed·Upper B / Thu·Lower B, visible lists differ per day → Save & Assign → SQL shows 4 distinct workouts rows (day_of_week 1-4) each with its own focus-matched exercises, zero overlap (SMOKE29A deleted, Alex intake restored). REMEDIATION NOTE: programs saved BEFORE this fix (e.g. Jonny's "Hypertrophy phase 1") keep their cloned rows — fix per-client via Load Saved → per-day edit, or regenerate; no mass-edit performed (fix/per-day-exercises)
- Generator minimum exercises per day (Phase 29B): every generated training day now ends with at least 8 exercises (`MIN_EXERCISES_PER_DAY` in src/lib/aiProgramGenerator.ts — the ONLY source file changed besides tests). Previously a slot was silently dropped when pickExercise returned null (category pool emptied by equipment/safety/experience filters — e.g. Jonny's 6-exercise days, and the 29A smoke's 2-exercise Lower B). Top-up per day after the slot loop: (1) cycle the day's own slotCategories again (pickExercise's same-category fallback handles repeats); (2) any exercise across the whole filtered pool, unused preferred; (3) the unused-preferred ordering degenerates to sensible repeats as a last resort. Extra exercises get order prefixes past the slot range (E1, E2…) so no A/B/C/D collisions. SAFETY HARDENING (same file, required by the "safety inviolable" rule): the new (c) test caught that applySafetyFilter could swap an excluded exercise for an alternative that is ITSELF exclude-matched (Deadlift → BB Deadlift - Blocks) — it now takes the first candidate that does not hit an exclude contraindication, else drops the exercise. NOTE: the Step 6 UI swap buttons intentionally keep the trainer-in-the-loop alternatives list unchanged (human reviews before swapping). Tests: new src/lib/aiProgramGenerator.test.ts (5) — Bodyweight Only + beginner fills every day to ≥8 (4-day and 3-day), Full Gym 4-day yields exactly 8 with zero cross-day repeats, safety-excluded never reappears after top-up, unique order prefixes. Verified: gates green (99 tests); smoke (Playwright) — regenerated for Jonny with his real (NULL → default) intake: 3 Full Body days, 8/8/8 exercises, distinct per day, focus-matched (Mon Pull+Legs: Pull up, Goblet Split Squat, Decline Close-grip Bench, BB Deadlift — Blocks); NOT saved per brief (feat/generator-min-exercises)
- Program Library page (Phase 28F): new trainer-only route `/library` (requireTrainer, same pattern as Coach) + sidebar item in the trainer branch of Layout's secondary nav. Grid of the 48 live program_templates (is_active only): name, category, up to 4 hashtag-derived chips (+N overflow), search box, tag-filter chips built from the real tag universe (frequency-sorted, toggle). Detail modal per template: full tag list, "Best-matched methods" and "Best-fit goals" (top 3 with scores via method_/goal_program_template_scores embedded joins on methods/goals; honest empty states), and a gradient "Use in Program Builder" button → `/ai-program-builder?template=<id>`. The wizard's new template effect prefills programName, description (first 3 humanized tags joined by ·), tags (first 5 raw), and best-effort goal/method via the pre-computed top-scored names mapped through TEMPLATE_GOAL_MAP/TEMPLATE_METHOD_MAP (regex maps in the new shared `src/lib/programTemplates.ts`, documented there — e.g. Conditioning→endurance, Circuit Conditioning→hiit; unmappable → wizard defaults stay). Unknown template id → toast + fresh wizard (same pattern as ?load=). Templates carry NO exercise content — generation fills that role via the existing flow. Type fixes (stale generated types, no schema change): program_templates block updated to the real 15-column shape (adds tags/is_active/display_order/etc.) and goal_program_template_scores added — required for the typed queries; the pre-existing UNUSED useMethodPrograms hook left untouched (wrong direction for this page — template→methods, not method→templates). NO fake content anywhere: description/duration are NULL in the catalog and are never shown. Verified: gates green (107 tests, 8 new for parse/humanize/mapping); smoke (Playwright) — 48 cards render, search GBC→1, GBC modal shows real scores (HIIT/Circuit/Interval 45.9, Conditioning 44.0/Fat Loss 40.6), Use in Program Builder lands with name prefilled + template-loaded toast (goal preselected via the tested mapping), tag filter narrows to 6, client role: sidebar item hidden + direct /library URL blocked by requireTrainer (feat/program-library)
- Wizard method browser (Phase 30A): Step 2 (Method Selection) is now DB-driven — the 39 active methods grouped under their 6 method_categories (display_order), replacing the 5 hardcoded METHODS with fake 87-95 scores. New shared `src/lib/methodCatalog.ts`: WIZARD_GOAL_TO_DB (documented wizard-goal → goals-table names map: hypertrophy→[Hypertrophy, Build Muscle], fatloss→[Fat Loss, Lose Weight], strength→[Strength], endurance→[Endurance, Conditioning], power→[] — no Power goal exists in the catalog, intentionally unranked, rehab→[Injury Rehab, Prehab / Rehab]), rankMethods (best goal_method_scores score across mapped goals, scored desc then unscored alphabetical), groupByCategory, resolveMethodName (DB slug → live name, legacy id → label, raw → humanized). 9 unit tests. The page fetches methods/categories/goals once and re-queries scores on goal change (graceful: any failure → unranked alphabetical, catalog failure → honest error box). Cards: name, category, tag chips (real hashtags — all 39 descriptions are NULL so none are fabricated; drawer shows an honest "No description in the catalog yet" note), real score when ranked, "Best Match" badge on #1 + green banner ("Best Match for Hypertrophy"). Card click opens a right slide-over drawer (full tags, score, Select This Method). Selection stores the DB slug in ProgramData.method; selectMethod also replaces the `AI Generated` placeholder tag with the method name. The old const survives as LEGACY_METHODS (display fallback for pre-30A values + METHOD_MAP auto-generate defaults, documented); METHOD_MAP itself unchanged and documented (legacy ids keep current behavior; DB slugs are metadata only — the generator never consumes data.method). Step 7/8 show the resolved DB method name via resolveMethodName. Type drift fixed in src/types/supabase.ts (no schema change): methods and method_categories blocks updated to the real live shapes (tags/is_active/category_id:int/display_order etc.). NOTE: methods.category TEXT column is unreliable (e.g. Interval Training says Classic Strength Protocols while category_id=2) — grouping uses category_id per the brief; the badge still shows the raw text. Verified: gates green (116 tests); smoke (Playwright) — 39 cards in 6 groups (group headers render uppercase via CSS — assertions made case-insensitive after first run), Hypertrophy ranks real scores + Best Match badge/banner, GVT drawer + Select, Preview + Save steps show "German Volume Training (10x10)", SMOKE30A persisted active for Alex then deleted (feat/wizard-method-browser)
- Wizard phase editor + phase persistence (Phase 30B): phase structure now survives save/load. ProgramPhase gains optional intensityTarget + volumeTarget (shown as amber/purple badges on cards). Step 4 (Phase Configuration): pencil per phase opens an edit dialog (name, weeks 1-16 clamped, focus, intensity/volume targets, 6-swatch color picker); Copy button duplicates with " (copy)" suffix inserted after the original; the fake "Recommend Phases" reset is replaced by a method-aware suggestion banner driven by the selected 30A method via new pure `src/lib/phaseSuggestions.ts` (documented map: GVT/10x10/GBC → 1×6w Accumulation; 5x5/stronglift → Foundation/Intensification; triphasic/conjugate → 3-phase block; hyphen-tolerant regexes match names and slugs; unknown → no banner; Accept applies, X dismisses; 4 unit tests). Persistence: buildProgramInsert writes the ACTIVE phases array (all fields) to programs.phases; programDataFromDb restores from it when present (shape-validated), legacy single-phase-from-duration fallback otherwise — lossless round-trip. Verified: gates green (120 tests); migration proof above; smoke (Playwright) — GVT method → banner "Accumulation (6w)" → Accept → edit (Accumulation+, RPE 7-8, MEV range) → duplicate → Save & Assign → SQL shows the 2-phase array with all fields → ?load= restores the full structure (NOT the single-phase fallback) → program deleted. NOTE: no Triphasic/Conjugate methods exist in the live catalog — the smoke exercises the suggestion engine via GVT (feat/wizard-phase-editor)
- Superset / circuit pairing engine (Phase 30C): method-aware pairing applied at the wizard layer (generator untouched). New pure `src/lib/supersets.ts`: pairingStyleForMethod (documented map — Supersets/GBC/GVT/10x10 → pairs, Trisets → triples, Circuits/Giant Sets → circuit, else null), assignPairGroups (A,A,B,B… / triples / all-A circuit, letters capped at H), stripPairing; 8 unit tests. handleAutoGenerate captures the selected 30A method BEFORE mapGeneratedToProgramData overwrites it and, when it has a pairing style, assigns groups to every day's list + toasts "Supersets configured for X"; methods without a style generate unchanged. ProgramExercise gains optional supersetGroup. Client ProgramsTab (read-only) shows the same linked chip next to exercise codes. Verified: gates green (128 tests); smoke (Playwright) — Supersets method → generate for Alex → pair badges on every day + Rest-after-group hints → pair control A→C re-groups → Save & Assign → SQL: notes jsonb contains supersetGroup (A/A, B/B, C/C, D/D) → ?load= restores the badges → program deleted. NOTE: the toast assertion in the smoke read too late (sonner fade) — the pairing path is proven by the badges and SQL (feat/superset-pairing)
- Progression rules + preview metrics (Phase 30D): PRE-APPROVED schema change applied live: `programs.progression_rules jsonb` nullable (supabase/program-progression-rules.sql, mirrored in schema.sql, types updated; information_schema-verified). STEP 1 — new pure `src/lib/progression.ts`: the 5 doc PRESETS (double/linear/deload/rest-pause/none with exact doc texts) + progressionNoteForWeek (deload weeks every 4th show the deload text, other weeks the load-rule text); ProgramData.progressionRules; Step 6 gains a rules editor below the safety section — preset multi-select chips + editable rules list (presets read-only, customs get label+text inputs, add/remove). buildProgramInsert writes rules (empty → null); programDataFromDb restores (shape-validated, fallback []). Rules render read-only on Step 7 (card), Step 8 (Final Review block), and the client ProgramsTab program detail (wired through ClientProfile mapper + ClientGeneratedProgram type). STEP 2 — new pure `src/lib/previewMetrics.ts`: setsPerMuscleGroup (exact name → EXERCISE_CATEGORIES index, unmatched → Other, documented), inferEquipment (documented regexes, dumbbell before barbell since dumbbell contains bb), equipmentChecklist (Full Gym/Dumbbells Only/Bodyweight Only/Home Gym coverage map; unassigned/no-intake → null + "Full Gym assumed" note; Other never verifiable), estimateSessionMinutes (sets × 40s + Σ rest). Step 7 Summary/Week-by-Week toggle: distribution bars (cyan→purple, sets + %), equipment checklist (✅/⚠️/➖), per-day time estimates, week cards with per-week progression notes (deload weeks amber). 13 unit tests across both libs. Verified: gates green (141 tests); smoke (Playwright) — generate for Jonny (intake temporarily 4-day/Full Gym/build_muscle, restored after) → Step 6 picks Double + Deload → Step 7 bars (POSTERIOR 18·15%, PRESSING 16·14%…) + checklist + per-day times → week-by-week 4 cards with the week-4 deload note → Save & Assign → SQL holds both rules → Load Saved restores rules + preview + ✅ checklist (assigned client) + deload note → program deleted. SMOKE LESSON: the checklist's ✅ requires an ASSIGNED client — auto-generate wipes assignedClient (pre-existing), so the unassigned preview correctly shows "Full Gym assumed" and ✅ was verified on the loaded program instead (feat/progression-preview)
- Exercise library seed (Phase 31A — DATA phase, no UI): exercise_library went from 0 to 458 rows, seeded from the in-code EXERCISE_CATEGORIES. New committed `scripts/seed-exercise-library.mjs` (zero deps, plain node): parses the categories out of src/data/exerciseDatabase.ts with a deterministic state machine (CRLF-safe), builds rows per the documented mapping — stable codes EX0001-EX0458 (category file order → exercises → alternatives, alphabetical within), slugified names with -2/-3 collision suffixes, primary_muscle per category (pressing→Chest, pulling→Back, quads→Quadriceps, posterior→Posterior Chain, delt_scap→Shoulders, arms→Biceps/Triceps, bracing+metcon→Core, target_areas→Full Body), secondary_muscle (pressing→Triceps, pulling→Biceps, posterior→Glutes, delt_scap→Upper Back), equipment via the 30D inferEquipment regexes (copied, no app imports), difficulty from the generator's beginner/extreme regexes, exercise_type (Isolation/Compound/Core), safety_notes from the 28D exclude contraindications ("Contraindicated: lower back pain" / "cardiovascular condition"), description/youtube/image/met NULL (honest), is_active true; joins to exercise_library_muscles/equipment SKIPPED (equipment_types has 0 rows, flat columns satisfy all constraints). Two modes: --emit-sql (used — generated supabase/seed-exercise-library.sql, applied via pooler) and REST upsert with SUPABASE_URL + SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY from env (no secrets in the script). IDEMPOTENT via ON CONFLICT (slug) DO UPDATE. Verified LIVE: 458 rows (per-category: pressing 60, pulling 44, bilateral_quad 34, unilateral_quad 33, posterior 43, target_areas 119, metcon_bracing 29, bracing 29, biceps 30, triceps 17, delt_scap 20); 0 NULL/empty in code/slug/name/equipment/primary_muscle; 0 duplicate slugs/codes; spot rows across categories correct (15° BB Incline Press=Barbell/Chest/Triceps/Compound, Prowler Sprint=Contraindicated: cardiovascular condition); re-apply → count unchanged 458; REST anon SELECT → 200 with rows. - Notifications crash + crash-robustness hotfix (Phase 33A; audit findings #1/#15/#16, both audits agreed): FIX 1 — src/pages/Notifications.tsx: saveSettings now persists ONLY {id, enabled, time} (icons never serialized) and loadSettings merges saved values onto DEFAULT_SETTINGS by id (icons always from code), which auto-repairs already-poisoned localStorage with no migration; unknown saved ids dropped, missing ids fall back to defaults, try/catch kept. 4 unit tests incl. the REAL production poison shape ({icon:{displayName:'Dumbbell'}} — note: lucide memo components ARE objects, so validity is asserted via 980typeof, not typeof). - Target-load cascade hotfix (Phase 33E; owner-reported multi-digit bug): updateExerciseTargetLoad's cascade only wrote sets with clientLoad <= 0, so typing a multi-digit target (50) cascaded the first digit ("5") and ignored the rest — Client Load showed 5 while the header/prescribed showed 50. New pure `cascadeTargetLoad(sets, prevTarget, nextTarget)` in workoutSession.ts: cascade follows the PREVIOUS target value — every unfinished set with clientLoad 0 or equal to the old target updates; manual divergences and done sets are never touched. 4 unit tests (0→50 two-keystroke follow-through, 50→55 preserving a manual 45, done sets untouched, diverged sets preserved). Verified: gates green (163 tests); smoke (Playwright, throwaway session, deleted after) 5/5 — type 50 → all five rows show 50 + prescribed @ 50kg, reload persists via the 33C entry-notes channel, manual 45 on row 3 then target 55 → rows 1/2/4/5 = 55, row 3 = 45, header + prescribed agree (fix/target-load-cascade)

RLS: the existing trainer ALL policy on trainer_id already covers these rows; new "Clients can view own-record sessions" SELECT policy (email match) covers clients who later create accounts. REST proofs: trainer insert 201, other-role select 0 rows, client insert for another client 403. ScheduleTab shows the SAME five day actions for account-less clients (no more activation dead-end) and loads sessions across both id spaces; useSessions embeds the clients name via client_record_id so /schedule + dashboard show Jonny's name; the dashboard returning-from-holiday logic matches either id. ITEM 3 (P2): ProgramsTab exercises get an inline pencil editor (sets / rep-range / tempo) writing the exercises row directly (tempo merged into the notes JSON with pct1RM/safety/superset keys preserved); exercise id + notesRaw threaded through the ClientProfile mapper + types. Logged data untouched by design (66 log entries before = after; the session player snapshots targets at session start). Verified: gates green (169 tests); smokes — ITEM 1: 2 sets ticked → Finish → RPE 7 → toast + summary; SQL: 6 entry rows + completed=true + duration + {"sessionRpe":7} (cleanup done). ITEM 2: Jonny (account-less) booked next Tue 09:00 via the tab → row with client_id NULL + client_record_id set → SQL read-back → deleted. ITEM 3: chin-up 4×8-12 → 4×6-8 + tempo 2-0-2-0 → reload persists → logs untouched → reverted (feat/session-and-scheduling)

- Nutrition Command Center (Phase 37): new `src/components/dashboard/NutritionCommandCenter.tsx` on the trainer dashboard directly below FollowUpsWidget — one row per client answering "how is every client eating right now?" from REAL queries only. DATA-SOURCE MAPPING: targets ← nutrition_targets (client→profile resolved via the established email match, 28C pattern); meal-plan status ← meal_plans via clients.id (covers account-less clients; item count = jsonb items array length); adherence ← nutrition_logs (real food-log table) as distinct logged_date in the last 7 days — profile-linked clients only; clients without a profile get the honest muted "No food logging yet" chip; no food-log fabrication anywhere. Sort via new pure `src/lib/nutritionCommand.ts` (nutritionDot red=no targets / amber=targets but no plan / green=plan saved; sortNutritionRows red→amber→green, alphabetical within, non-mutating; 6 unit tests). Header badge "X of Y with targets · Z with plans"; widget hidden entirely when the trainer has zero clients; loading renders nothing (FollowUpsWidget pattern); query failure shows an honest "Couldn't load" line. RLS verified: trainer-read policies exist on all three tables. Theme lock: CSS vars throughout, FollowUpsWidget visual language, no hardcoded page chrome. - Nutrition Adherence Loop (Phase 38): plan → one-tap log → weekly adherence, all on real nutrition_logs. ITEM 1 (log-from-plan) — MealPlanCard gains per-meal "Log" actions: plan items store NO food id (27F saves name as `name`/`name (brand)`), so new pure `resolvePlanFood` in mealPlan.ts exact-matches both forms (trimmed/case-insensitive, first match wins, null → skip, never guesses; 3 new tests); handleLogMeal inserts one nutrition_logs row per resolved item (meal_type 1:1 — DB CHECK is breakfast/lunch/dinner/snacks, quantity_g ← serving_g, logged_date = client-local today) with toasts "Logged N items to X ✅" / "N skipped — not in food database", a "Logged ✓" chip per meal, and re-log allowed ("Log again"). RLS DEVIATION (reported, not worked around): nutrition_logs INSERT is own-rows-only ("Users can insert own nutrition_logs"), so trainers CANNOT log on a client's behalf — the Log action renders only on the client's own Nutrition page via a new `canLog` prop (never passed by the trainer NutritionTab); making trainer-logging work needs an owner-approved policy change. Also gated on canLog (client view is read-only + log): Generate/Regenerate/Swap/Save stay trainer-side because meal_plans INSERT is trainer-only ("Trainers can manage meal plans" — clients get an honest "your trainer can create one" empty state instead of unsavable drafts). ITEM 2 (weekly strip) — new `src/components/nutrition/WeeklyAdherenceStrip.tsx` (userId prop, same component both views): self-fetches its own nutrition_targets row (distinguishes "no targets" from the DEFAULT_TARGETS fallback → honest "Set targets first" instead of fake %) + ONE 7-day logs query; renders n/7 days logged, a 7-bar div mini-chart (cyan→purple bars, dashed amber target line, no new deps — recharts exists but divs match the app's MacroBar idiom), and avg kcal/protein vs target %. Pure logic in `src/lib/weeklyAdherence.ts` (last7Dates local-date-safe incl. month boundaries, aggregateWeek, targetPercents; averages over LOGGED days only — documented; 8 tests). Mounted on the client Nutrition page (refreshKey prop refetches after any log/copy/remove — first smoke run caught the stale-mount) and REPLACES the trainer tab's old "7-Day Adherence" card (which ran 7 sequential getDayTotals queries). ITEM 3 (copy yesterday) — client page button duplicates yesterday's rows into today (same food_id/meal_type/quantity_g), toast with count, hidden when yesterday has zero rows or a non-today date is viewed. BUG FIXED IN PASSING (required for Item 1 visibility): Nutrition.tsx used meal type "snack" (singular) while the DB CHECK only allows "snacks" — every snack log was silently dropped from the client page; now "snacks" everywhere on the page. Client page also mounts MealPlanCard (it was never on the client page before — brief premise corrected) with intake_profile diet/restrictions resolved. Verified: gates green (187 tests, 11 new); smoke (Playwright, 18/18) — trainer saved a 13-item test plan for Alex → client logged breakfast (SQL: exactly 3 rows, matching the button's item count) → Logged chip + Log again → strip 1/7 + 19% kcal / 42% protein → Copy yesterday hidden when empty, then modal-logged 1 item to yesterday → button appeared → copy → SQL today = 3+1 → trainer reload shows the SAME strip (2/7) with zero Log buttons → full cleanup (test logs + test plan deleted, SQL-verified zero leftovers). Screenshots: .temp/audit/shots/nutrition-adherence-{client,trainer}.png (feat/nutrition-adherence)
- Diet-aware macros + staple food database (Phase 39): ITEM 1 (diet splits) — calculateMacroTargets (28E D6) previously ignored diet except veg/vegan; now, AFTER the unchanged protein-first ladder: low_carb caps carbs at ~20% of total kcal with fats absorbing the remainder; high_carb caps fats at ~20% of total kcal (NEVER below the 0.6 g/kg / female-25% floor) with carbs absorbing the remainder; high_protein adds +0.2 g/kg LBM to the ladder (still under the 35% kcal cap and the kidney 1.6 cap); balanced/undefined/vegetarian/vegan are byte-identical to pre-39 (no-drift test proves it). Documented corner: at extremely low kcal where protein + fat floor exceed the target, low_carb keeps kcal consistent and lets fats sit under the floor (the pre-39 code overspent kcal there instead). DIET_PRESETS gains vegetarian + vegan entries (balanced split — their effect is the 1.8 ladder) so BOTH diet dropdowns (TdeeCalculator + 33D intake wizard, both map over DIET_PRESETS) now offer all 6 keys; the wizard already forwarded diet into calculateNutritionPipeline and persists it to intake_profile. Callers verified: TdeeCalculator passes diet (useMemo deps include it) and the wizard pipeline forwards it — no wiring was broken. Generator: new pure rankFoodPool (mealPlan.ts) RANKS instead of filters — tier 1) source='seed-staples' first, 2) snack slots prefer fruit/snacks/dairy categories, 3) diet-fit metric (low_carb → carb-density asc, high_carb → desc, else protein-density desc), name tiebreak; generateMealPlan pools are ranked slices CAPPED at the staple count so non-staple rows only fill in when staples can't reach the minimum pool of 4 (zero staples → pre-39 natural slices, legacy DBs unaffected); MealPlanCard.ensureFoods selects source and orders source DESC so the 500-row cap can't crowd staples out. ITEM 2 (staple seed) — new scripts/seed-staple-foods.mjs (zero-dep, regenerable) emits supabase/seed-staple-foods.sql: 98 honest USDA-FDC per-100g staples (protein 21 incl. chicken/turkey/lean beef/salmon/tuna/eggs/tofu/tempeh/seitan/whey 30g-scoop, carbs 22, fruit 12, vegetables 15, fats 14, dairy 10, snacks 4; whey + protein bar use realistic unit servings), controlled category set, source='seed-staples', slug source_ids, no brands. SCHEMA NOTE (required by the pre-approved design, which names source='seed-staples'): foods_cache_source_check only allowed ('off','custom') — widened to include 'seed-staples' via the seed migration (CHECK-list expansion, NO column changes; mirrored in supabase/schema.sql). Applied live via pooler: 98 rows; zero null/empty/≤0-kcal rows; zero duplicate slugs; re-apply → count unchanged (idempotent); the 57 OFF-imported rows untouched (not deleted — user data). ITEM 3 (reactivity) — unit tests prove gender/age/height/weight/activity/goal each move BMR/TDEE/targets in the correct direction through the full pipeline; smoke confirms the UI recalculates (expected none broken — none were). Verified: gates green (208 tests, +21); smoke (Playwright, 9/9) — TdeeCalculator on Alex: balanced P128/C418/F62 → low_carb P128/C137/F187 → high_carb C420/F61 → high_protein P141 (all @2740, visibly distinct), sex male→female 2740→2483, age 30→55 2740→2547; post-seed plan regenerate → zero ice-cream/mayo/candy → save → SQL: 13/13 items ARE seed-staples → test plan deleted (SQL-verified). Screenshots: .temp/audit/shots/diet-aware-{tdee,plan}.png. KNOWN LIMITATION (pre-existing, out of scope): the generator optimizes kcal per meal slice, not per-meal macro targets — a fat-loss plan can land under the protein target (smoke plan: P156/206); a macro-target-aware generator is a future phase (feat/diet-aware-nutrition)
- Macro-accurate multi-day meal plans (Phase 40): the 27E random-pick generator is replaced at the card level by template-driven construction + a fit engine. ITEM 1 — new pure `src/lib/mealPlanV2.ts`: MEAL_TEMPLATES table (6 breakfasts / 6 lunches / 5 dinners / 5 snacks modeling the canonical plate combos — yogurt bowl, eggs+toast, proats, cottage+fruit, smoothie, muffin+eggs; chicken+rice, salmon+quinoa, turkey wrap, tuna+potato, bean burrito, tofu stir-fry; salmon+sweet potato, beef+potato, stir-fry, chicken+pasta, prawn+rice; 5 snacks) with slot roles protein/carb/vegfruit/fat/dairy filled from preferred food names with category fallbacks (restrictions/diet filtering unchanged via filterFoods — extended MEAT_KEYWORDS with cod/haddock/mackerel/tilapia/trout, a 27E gap that let whole fish into veg plans). Slots sized protein-first (per-meal protein shares 25/30/30/15%, breakfast floored at 25 g best-effort), then carb, fat, veg/fruit filler; realistic per-food rounding (eggs 50 g, whey 30 g scoop, protein bar 60 g, wrap 60 g, bagel 85 g, muffin 45 g, bread 40 g slice, rice cakes 10 g, meat/fish 10 g, dairy 25 g, else 5 g) with per-role max servings. ITEM 2 — fitDayToTargets: steepest-descent hill-climb over ±step/±4×step single-item moves + top-up moves (add olive oil ≤40 g to a meal / whey ≤60 g) minimizing a band-deviation score (kcal ±5%, protein 90–115%, carbs/fats ±15%); monotone, deterministic, ≤300 iterations, best-attempt + honest accuracy on stall. accuracyOf → {kcalPct, proteinPct, carbsPct, fatsPct}. ITEM 3 — generateMultiDayPlan (1–7 days) with variety: no identical slot-food-set twice per meal, no protein repeat on consecutive days per meal type, per-day deterministic seeds; regenerateDay rebuilds ONE day with the same constraints vs both neighbors. Persistence additive: items gain optional day + role (old plans render as day 1, unchanged); meal_plans.targets jsonb gains an accuracy report (plan-level average). MealPlanCard: day-count selector (1/3/5/7, default 5) on generate, Day 1..N tabs (gradient active pill), per-day accuracy chip (never rounded to 100), amber warnings line, per-day Regenerate-day + swap (same-category, kcal-parity, step-rounded), 38's log-from-plan logs the SELECTED day's meal (resolvePlanFood unchanged — names kept). ITEM 4 — seed top-up: +5 staples (Black Beans, Feta, Mixed Berries, Cauliflower Rice, Wholemeal English Muffin) via the 39 generator → 103 rows live (idempotent re-apply verified). Verified: gates green (227 tests, +19 — template-vs-seed-name integrity test parses the real SQL, 5 scenario tolerance matrix incl. low_carb/high_protein, fat-loss undershoot regression, rounding, variety, determinism, regenerateDay, veg no-meat); smoke (Playwright, 12/12) — 7-day plan for Alex: all days distinct, accuracy chips 95–100% kcal / 98–114% protein EVERY day, SQL: days 1–7 populated, every item resolves to foods_cache, zero consecutive-protein repeats, zero identical meals; client logged Day-3 lunch (exactly its 4 items, no other meal rows); Day-5 swap works (Skyr → Protein Yogurt); full cleanup SQL-verified. Screenshots: .temp/audit/shots/mealplan-v2-{tabs,client-log}.png. NOTE (demo data observation, not from this phase): Alex's stored nutrition_targets are the LOW_CARB split (2740/128/137/187) — residue from July-23-era testing (his intake diet is null/balanced; the 38 screenshots showed 206/240/107). Legitimate 28E output either way; the smoke fitting the brutal 187 g-fat day at 95–100% kcal is the hardest-case proof. Owner may re-apply balanced targets via the calculator if desired. Old generateMealPlan stays exported (its tests untouched) but nothing in the app calls it anymore (feat/meal-plan-v2)

EDT: header countdown chip (15:00, click to start/pause, toast "Block complete — log your rounds" at 0:00, once). Methods without defaults → player unchanged. ITEM 3 (rest + haptics) — parseRestSeconds (ranges take the midpoint, "60–90s"→75, "2–3 min"→150, unparseable→null, documented); marking a set done starts the timer with the method's default rest ONLY when the exercise row has no explicit rest (new SessionExercise.hasExplicitRest from buildSessionExercise — row value always wins); rest-pause slug: a set done at the top of the rep range presets 15s intra-set rest. Haptics: src/lib/haptics.ts (safeVibrate feature-detects navigator.vibrate + localStorage toggle azfit_rest_haptics, default ON) wired into useRestTimer (buzz at 10s left, double-buzz at 0:00) + a Vibrate toggle button in the player header. Verified: gates green (270 tests, +8); smoke (Playwright, 13/13) — GVT session started → header badge + "Set 1 of 10" → set done (60kg×10) → "Set 2 of 10" → finish → SQL completed → timestamps shifted to yesterday → reopened session shows "Last: 60 kg × 10" matching SQL exactly → NULL-rest row starts the timer at ~1:15 (75s GVT midpoint) → haptics toggle writes + persists localStorage → client login sees the same ghost line → EDT session shows the 15:00 chip → all logs/entries deleted, both programs archived, SQL-verified. Vibrate itself can't be asserted headless — the guard + toggle persistence are what's tested (documented). Screenshots: .temp/audit/shots/workout-intel-{ghost,edt}.png (feat/workout-intelligence)

- Auto-progression: rule-based progressive-overload suggestions (Phase 62 — deterministic, NO LLM, advisory tap-to-apply only): the workout player now suggests today's working weight from REAL logged history. STEP 0 findings (documented): the schema HAS rpe_per_set (RPE exists) but the rules are deliberately reps-completion-only (no RPE branching this phase); the 49 ghost query selected entries newest-first WITHOUT workout_log_id (extended, not duplicated); SessionExerciseCard applies loads via onUpdateSet(setIndex, {clientLoad, load}) — the chip uses exactly that path; parseRepRange did not exist anywhere (parseTargetReps takes the first number only). ITEM 1 (engine, src/lib/autoProgression.ts — named so because src/lib/progression.ts already exists (Phase 30D program progression rules — a collision I introduced and fixed; the 30D module + its tests are intact): progressionSuggestion — INCREASE when the latest session logged ≥ prescribedSets sets ALL at one weight ALL at repRangeMax (increment: +2.5 kg when the working weight is < 40 kg — documented upper/isolation heuristic since the player has no reliable exercise-level equipment metadata in scope — else +5 kg; single increment from the NEWEST weight even when the previous session also topped out — no compounding); DECREASE when ≥2 sets missed repRangeMin (−5% rounded to nearest 2.5, guarded > 0 and < working weight); HOLD otherwise (repeat working weight + short reason); NULL on no usable history / zero weights / unparseable range — never fabricate. parseRepRange ("8-10"/"8–10"→min/max, "10"→10/10, "AMRAP"→0/0), roundToPlateau (nearest 2.5, floor 2.5), sessionsByExercise (group ghost rows into ≤2 newest sessions per exercise via the log's completed_at order). 16 unit tests: full-top increase both increment tiers, boundary rounding, partial-top hold with exact reason text, miss decrease, single-set miss no-decrease, no compounding, prescribedSets mismatch, varying weights no-increase, newest-session-only verdict, null paths, grouping end-to-end. ITEM 3 (data wiring): the hook's ghost query now selects workout_log_id + orders completed logs by completed_at desc (SAME two queries — zero extra round-trips); the progression map computes per exercise from parseRepRange(targetReps) + targetSets. ITEM 2 (chip): SessionExerciseCard renders "Suggested: N kg — tap to apply" ONLY for increase/decrease with a suggestedWeight (hold/null render nothing — the ghost line already implies repeat); increase = brand cyan, decrease = warning amber (NOT an AI surface — no violet); tap applies via the focused set (or first incomplete set) through the existing onUpdateSet mechanics, never auto-submits; reason in the chip's title tooltip. SMOKE-CAUGHT FIX (pre-existing, same class as the 58 clients bug): the session player's sticky header pushed the page to 447px at 390px viewport — title block couldn't shrink; fixed with min-w-0 + truncate (now 390/390). Verified: gates green (tsc / lint / test 420 = 404+16 new / build); smoke 11/11 — seeded scenario (program + 2 exercises 3×8-10 + completed session squat 3×60×10 → "Suggested: 65 kg" with the exact engine reason, bench 3×50×[9,9,8] → hold renders NO chip) → tap prefills a weight input with 65 → client login sees the identical chip → light theme + 390px clean → zero console errors → all seeded rows (program, workout, exercises, logs, entries, in-progress player log) SQL-verified deleted. Real-data examples: 3×(60,10)@8-10 → increase 65; 3×(50,[9,9,8])@8-10 → hold (nothing rendered). Screenshots: .temp/audit/shots/62/ (player-chips-dark, player-applied-dark, player-client-dark, player-390-light) (feat/auto-progression)

The header row was also a 4-column grid that never matched the flex rows. - Calendar/Schedule overhaul (Owner Tasks, Task 4 of feat/session-nav-polish): (a) MOBILE: /schedule now defaults to the Day/agenda view below 768px (Week toggle kept; tablet/desktop keep the 7-column week grid) and the day selector defaults to TODAY (was always Monday); the client-profile month grid got the cramped-layout pass (smaller mobile cell padding/min-height, leading-tight chips). (b) TITLES: event titles are now '{ClientName} PT' — BookSessionDialog generates the new form and supabase/fix-session-titles.sql renamed the 7 existing 'PT with …' rows live (verified: 'Ben Sabre PT' etc.). (c) Tap a tile → the Task 2 detail modal (already shipped; re-verified on mobile). (d) DRAG-AND-DROP rescheduling: pure math in src/lib/scheduleDnd.ts (snapMinutesToSlot at the 30-min Book-Session increment, minutesToTimeString, duration-preserving movedSessionTimes, dropAllowed start+end availability check — 8 unit tests). Desktop/tablet: native HTML5 drag of a session tile with the target cell highlighted live (ghost preview; pointer-Y picks the :00/:30 half-slot). Mobile: press-and-hold ~300ms picks the tile up (a NON-PASSIVE native touchmove listener takes over — React's root listeners are passive and can't block scroll mid-drag; scroll intent before 300ms cancels the pickup), a floating ghost follows the finger with the snapped time, release snaps to the nearest slot, and the post-drag synthetic click is swallowed so the detail modal doesn't pop. Drops persist via rescheduleSession (optimistic + rollback) with availability (start AND end) and conflict guards. framer-motion gotcha documented: motion.div owns onDragStart/onDragEnd for its gesture API — EventCard is now a plain draggable div with a motion inner for the pop-in. PRE-EXISTING BUG flagged (not fixed, out of scope): formatDateKeyUtc uses UTC date parts, so at UTC+8 the week/day grid's cell date keys are the local day MINUS one — events render one visual day later than their local date (booking has the same shift; the grid is self-consistent so DnD works within its own frame; owner should confirm their timezone/impact before a fix). Verified: gates green (tsc / lint / test 451 = 443+8 new / build); smoke 14/14 + a 360px pass — '{Name} PT' titles, desktop HTML5 drag 09:00→14:00 with highlight + SQL-verified persist + persists after refresh, mobile Day default, press-hold ghost visible + snapped drop to 16:00 SQL-verified, tap opens detail, scrollWidth == viewport at 390 AND 360, zero console errors, SQL-verified cleanup
- Global back/forward arrows on every page (Owner Tasks, Task 5 of feat/session-nav-polish): ARCHITECTURE FINDING: only 5 surfaces self-wrap in the full Layout shell (Dashboard via DashboardRouter, Analytics, Clients, Coach, Library) — every other protected page renders bare inside ProtectedRoute, so HistoryNav (which lived only in Layout) was missing app-wide. Fix: new src/components/ArrowsShell.tsx (slim arrows-only bar with the existing HistoryNav, NON-sticky so page-level sticky headers on Schedule/ClientProfile keep their behavior) mounted as TWO nested layout routes in App.tsx covering all bare protected routes (group A /coach-ai…/client/:id, group B /warmup…/form-checks) — zero per-page edits; the 5 Layout pages, the 4 print routes (their own .no-print toolbar Back stays by design), and public routes stay outside. HISTORY BUG FIXED (root cause of 'Android back exits/dumps to Dashboard'): HistoryNav only wrote its sessionStorage stack inside the new-path branch — the initial push was never persisted, so every navigation re-seeded the stack at pointer 0 and Back was permanently disabled. Reworked: pure advanceHistory extracted to src/lib/historyStack.ts (7 unit tests incl. a real out-of-bounds pointer bug the cap-at-50 test caught — the pointer wasn't adjusted after the slice), render-time availability derived by SIMULATING the current navigation (the old render read the persisted pointer and lagged one navigation), persistence in an effect. Replace-navigations audited: only login/logout/signup use replace (legitimate — they should clear the stack); React Router handles the hardware-back popstate natively once in-app history exists. Verified: gates green (tsc / lint / test 458 = 451+7 new / build); smoke 11/11 — exactly one arrows bar per page (dashboard, client profile, schedule, nutrition), Dashboard→Clients→client detail→Schedule then Back steps in reverse to Dashboard and Forward returns to Clients, print pages have no shell bar (their toolbar Back remains), no h-scroll at 390, zero console errors
- Autosave + session restore (Owner Tasks, Task 6 of feat/session-nav-polish): new src/lib/draftStore.ts (localStorage, namespaced azfit-draft: keys, debounced ~1s coalescing writes, clearDraft cancels pending writes, draftIsNewer, formatDraftTime; 7 unit tests with fake timers) + shared src/components/DraftBanner.tsx ('You have unsaved changes from {time}.' [Resume] [Discard] — non-blocking, theme tokens). FOUR surfaces wired: (1) WORKOUT PLAYER — the critical one: typed-but-unfinished sets are local-state-only (updateSet never hits the DB), so the draft is the only copy; useActiveWorkoutSession autosaves the full exercises state once dirty, and on load offers resume when the log is unfinished. SCHEMA FINDING (documented, not changed): workout_logs.completed_at has DEFAULT NOW() — every log is 'completed' at INSERT (SessionLauncher's completed_at: undefined is dropped by supabase-js), so completed_at is useless as a finish marker; the player uses duration_minutes != null (set only by finishSession) as the honest finish signal. FLAG FOR OWNER: this default means started-but-abandoned workouts count as completed everywhere completed_at is read (55 compliance, 62 progression history) — changing it is a data-semantics decision deferred to the owner. PRE-EXISTING BUG found + documented (not fixed): toggleSetDone's per-set DB write is a silent no-op — React 19 doesn't run the setState updater eagerly, so exerciseAfterUpdate is null at the await; finishSession's flush is the only real save path (which is exactly why Task 6 matters). (2) BOOK SESSION wizard — single in-flight draft (book-session key), autosaves once non-pristine (create mode only — edit mode always prefills from the row), banner inside the dialog, cleared on successful booking. (3) PROGRAM BUILDER — program-builder key, autosaves data+currentStep once diverged from defaults, banner above the stepper, Resume restores data AND the exact step, cleared on Save/Assign/Reset. (4) PLAN SUMMARY editor — plan-summary-{clientId} key inside BlueprintForm (remounts per open), dirty-guard vs the prefilled inputs, cleared on generate. Verified: gates green (tsc / lint / test 465 = 458+7 new / build); smoke 13/13 — start workout, type set 1 (111 kg, local-only) + mark set 2 done (80), KILL the tab, reopen → banner → Resume restores both sets on the same screen (inputs 111/80 + done row), Finish flushes [0,80,0,0] to workout_log_entries and clears the draft (reopen → no banner), wizard notes survive Cancel→reopen with Resume restoring step 3 + text and Discard clearing permanently, zero console errors, SQL-verified cleanup
- Light & dark contrast audit (Owner Tasks, Task 7 of feat/session-nav-polish): ROOT CAUSE of the dark-theme leaks: [data-theme="dark"] never overrode the --light-elevated/--light-border/--light-text-muted tokens, which are used app-wide as generic elevated/muted aliases — so dark mode rendered near-white #F1F5F9 surfaces with the (dark-overridden) near-white --page-text on top = the 'white pill, invisible text' Today-tile rows and the near-white selected Clients row. Fixed at the TOKEN layer (src/index.css): dark theme now maps --light-elevated→var(--dark-elevated), --light-border→var(--dark-border), --light-text-muted→var(--dark-text-secondary). LIGHT-theme fixes: --light-text-muted #94A3B8 (2.5:1 on white, failed AA) → #64748B (4.7:1) — repairs muted text + placeholders app-wide; NEW --ring-track token (#64748B light / 45% muted-mix dark) drives the PulseRing track SOLID (the old opacity-20 + --light-border ring was invisible at 0% in light); NEW --ai-violet-strong (#5F4CB8 light / var(--ai-violet) dark) for violet TEXT on AI surfaces (--ai-violet itself is 2.9:1 on the light glass); AIPanel's glass bg switched from --dark-elevated 55% (a dark panel even in light theme!) to theme-aware --card-bg 55%. 'No targets — Set targets' + NCC dots/accents moved off #EF4444/#22C55E/#F59E0B to var(--danger)/var(--success)/var(--warning) (the 58 light semantics are AA on white); same token swap across AIInsightsPanel, ClientHealthGrid, FollowUpsWidget, RevenueSnapshot, SessionsRemainingCard, TodaysMealsCard, TrainerDashboard (incl. a #0D9488 teal leftover → var(--azfit-primary)), ScheduleTab + WorkoutLogsTab status maps (the `${color}20` alpha-suffix pattern is invalid with var() — now color-mix 12%), ClientDashboard/ProgramsTab teal rgba tints → color-mix brand cyan, ProtectedRoute loading screen (#0F172A always-dark + teal spinner → --page-bg + brand). FULL TEAL PURGE: every #0D9488 / rgba(13,148,136,*) leftover in src/ (26 files) → var(--azfit-primary)/color-mix — the documented teal leftover is gone from app code (index.css token comments keep the history). DECISION (brief-sanctioned): the booking/detail/block modals stay intentionally always-dark navy — measured 13.9:1 title contrast in light theme; tokens on those surfaces would flip to light-theme semantic values that fail on navy. NOT done (documented): the Schedule page itself remains an always-dark slate surface (not in the owner's leak list; a theme-aware migration is a separate visual decision). Verified: gates green (tsc / lint / test 465 unchanged / build); smoke 6/6 with COMPUTED WCAG ratios — Today row dark 14.1:1, Clients selected row dark 14.1:1 (bg now #1C2632), ring track solid #64748B in light, Coach AI header 6.3:1 in light, Book modal title 13.9:1 in light, zero console errors (today-session row SQL-seeded SMOKE-T7 + deleted). Screenshots: .temp/audit/shots/t7/ (dashboard-dark, dashboard-light, clients-dark-selected, book-modal-light, client-schedule-light)
- Program Creator bug fixes (Phase 65A, feat/phase-65a-program-creator-fixes): STEP 5 SYNC FIX — applySplit/toggleDay used to swap day labels without touching the per-day exercise map (the literal "Barbell Row on a Push day" repro: a stale Full-Body rotation surviving a PPL switch); both now route through the new reconcileWorkoutExercises primitive (src/lib/splitSeeder.ts) — result keys are exactly the new split's active days, a weekday whose label is unchanged keeps its list (trainer edits + dbIds survive), anything else is re-seeded pattern-aware and deterministic from the hardcoded category pools (no Math.random); preserveSplitIds stops the silent delete+re-insert of ALL workout rows on the next save (workout_logs.workout_id links preserved); the Split Type dropdown is now DERIVED from data.split (used to hardcode 'Upper/Lower' via local state); fresh wizards seed per-day lists from the start (defaultData), killing the Day-1-copy fallback duplication. GENERATOR FIXES (src/lib/aiProgramGenerator.ts): pickExercise is now tiered (unused-this-week → unused-today → documented same-category last resort — the old fallback could repeat a name WITHIN one day), and the 29B top-up re-cycles ONLY the day's own slotCategories (the old stage 2 padded from the whole pool and put pulls/hinges on Push days; a full day is never bought with a pattern violation). CHANGE-EXERCISE UX (Step 6): new ExerciseChangeDialog (Similar / Swap from day / Custom) — Similar ranks the live exercise_library via the new src/lib/exerciseTaxonomy.ts (52B muscle vocabulary → push/pull/legs/core patterns; 52A name normalization + equipment-stripped fallback matching bridges legacy names like 'Back Squat' ↔ 'BB Back Squat'); when the current exercise is flagged day-incompatible, Similar honestly switches to a day-fitting list instead of 'similar' wrong-pattern moves; Swap-from-day exchanges whole rows across days (swapExerciseAcrossDays in wizardSplit.ts); Custom hands off to the existing 31B picker. Pattern chips: rows whose library-classified pattern doesn't fit the day label get an amber "Pull on Push day"-style chip that opens the dialog (advisory only; unknown names stay silent — never accuse without a library basis). AI Auto-Fill now reseeds the selected day pattern-aware (used to reset every day to the same 6 hardcoded exercises). New runtime hook src/hooks/useExerciseTaxonomy.ts (one public-read query, module-cached — same table/RLS the 31B picker already used; zero schema/type/migration changes). KNOWN LIMITATIONS (documented): Step 7's Workouts card still previews the frozen auto-generated snapshot; toggling a day off discards its list (re-activation reseeds); exerciseSwap.ts untouched (its other consumers stay on the hardcoded path).
- Wizard UX: view modes, light theme, mobile-first, nav & info (Phase 65B, feat/wizard-ux-65b): ITEM 1 (light theme) — ROOT CAUSE of every dark leak: the wizard page root forced data-theme="dark" (same pattern family as the Schedule dark-only flag); wrapper removed, the page is now theme-native and verified legible on all 8 steps + modals in both themes. Same audit caught a 65A LATENT BUG: day-pattern keyword precedence put 'full' after 'legs'/'upper', so generated labels like 'Full Body 1 — Pull + Legs' classified as legs-only days (wrong chips + wrong seed pools) — 'full' now wins in dayPatternsForLabel AND splitSeeder LABEL_SLOTS (+2 regression tests). ITEM 2 (Windows-Explorer view switcher) — new src/lib/viewMode.ts (pure: 5 modes, localStorage wizardView.<page>, grid classes) + useViewMode hook + ViewModeSwitch icon group; all 5 modes (large/medium/small/list/details) on Goal Selection, Method Selection, Phase Configuration, Exercise Review; LARGE tiles get a BRANDED GRADIENT header from the accent + theme tokens (documented no-photo fallback — public/ has no per-goal/method imagery; never hotlinked); method tiles gain defaults chips (setsReps / frequencyPerWeek / durationWeeks from the Phase 48 jsonb — 'number of exercises' has no honest source in defaults, omitted not fabricated). ITEM 3 (mobile-first) — the 9-column Exercise Review table squeezed to unreadability at 390px; rows now stack below sm (line 1 code/name/actions, line 2 stats) with the desktop grid + header hidden, name cell/actions shared (no duplication); a live overflow hunt found list/details tiles blowing out to 503px on a long custom-goal name (flex min-content quirk on <button>) — fixed with w-full + min-w-0 across goal/method/phase rows; scrollWidth = 390 asserted on EVERY step. ITEM 4 (nav) — WizardNavBar shared component mounted at top AND bottom of every step (identical validation); ScrollArrows floating up/down buttons appear after 300px scroll, smooth-scroll, mobile-safe offset. ITEM 5 (method enrichment) — method drawer gains an Example-week panel (src/lib/methodWeek.ts: frequency spread + preferredCategories rotation + real prescription text, curated presentation) + 'Recommended for (guidance)' + CLIENT HISTORY (src/lib/methodHistory.ts: programs carrying phases[].method = slug → workouts → workout_logs → Σ weight×reps; sessions completed + volume change first→last with honest nulls; Generic-template / no-history / loading states — brief correction: workout_logs has NO program_id column, the chain goes via workouts). 18 new tests (513 total).

- Exercise library dedup SAFE-tier merge (Phase 72, feat/library-dedup-72): DATA-ONLY phase (no app code changed). Parsed the AUTHORITATIVE '## SAFE to auto-merge' table from docs/exercise-dedup-report.md (101 pairs — report predates the library growing 628 → 642 rows) via new scripts/dedup-exercises.mjs, which validates every pair against the LIVE DB (both codes exist + names match — mismatches skip, never force; zero skips needed) and emits supabase/dedup-exercises.sql: ONE transaction, one existence-guarded DO block per pair (idempotent — second run is a no-op, proven). STEP 1 FK DISCOVERY (pg_constraint, logged): exercise_library_muscles + exercise_library_equipment (composite-PK junctions, ON DELETE CASCADE) and trial_assessment_items (no cascade — repointed). ## Phase 64 — SPA deep-link fallback + UTC date-shift fix + event tile tokens + tile-click modal assertion

**Branch:** `fix/spa-routing-dates-64` off `main` (`b7bef4b`)  
**Goal:** Fix three verified live-site issues: GitHub Pages 404 on non-root URLs, UTC date-shift in schedule calendar, and hardcoded slate event tiles; add explicit Playwright tile-click→modal assertion.

### Item 1 — SPA deep-link fallback (`404.html`)
- Added `scripts/copy-404.mjs` to copy `dist/index.html` → `dist/404.html` after the Vite build.
- Wired as `"postbuild": "node scripts/copy-404.mjs"` inside the existing `build` npm script.
- `dist/404.html` is byte-identical to `dist/index.html` after build.
- Deploy mechanism: `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` builds `dist/` and uploads it via `actions/upload-pages-artifact@v3` / `actions/deploy-pages@v4`, so `dist/404.html` is published.
- Local smoke: `npx vite preview`, direct load `/schedule` returns the app shell (HTTP 200) instead of a 404 page.
- **Deviation / note:** The app uses `HashRouter`, so a direct load of `/schedule` still lands without a hash. The 404 fallback ensures the SPA shell loads; HashRouter then renders the default route. End users should share/bookmark hash URLs (`/#/schedule`) for deep links. Converting path-based deep links to hash links would require a redirect script in `404.html` and is out of scope for this phase.

### Item 2 — UTC date-shift fix
- Changed `src/hooks/useSessions.ts` `getDateKey()` from `toISOString().split('T')[0]` to `formatDateKeyLocal(d)` so `todaySessions` and `weekSessions` filter by the user's local calendar day.
- Changed all human-visible day keying in `src/pages/Schedule.tsx` from `formatDateKeyUtc` to `formatDateKeyLocal`:
  - `sessionToEvent` date key
  - `weekEvents` week-date filter
  - `dayEvents` selected-day filter
  - `stats` week-date filter
  - `WeekGrid` cell `dateKey`
  - `DayGrid` cell `dateKey`
  - `handleRepeatWeekly` storage-date construction (uses local date string to build the local `Date` that is then stored as ISO)
- Changed default date inputs in `src/components/schedule/BookSessionDialog.tsx` from `new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]` to `formatDateKeyLocal(new Date())` so new bookings use the local day and don't mismatch the cell-keyed availability check.
- `useHabits.ts` keeps `formatDateKeyUtc` because `habit_logs.log_date` is stored as a UTC date string; storage/compare operations remain UTC.
- Added `src/lib/utils.test.ts` with fixed `TZ=Asia/Shanghai`: asserts `2026-09-01T22:30:00Z` keys to `2026-09-02` locally and remains `2026-09-01` in UTC.
- **Verification:** Session `4e01d4e4-4477-4f38-8631-cc04e1674966` (`2026-08-30T22:30:00Z`) now renders on **Mon Aug 31** (local +8) instead of Sun Aug 30.

### Item 3 — Event tile token polish
- Added CSS variables to `src/index.css` for both light and dark themes:
  `--event-tile-bg`, `--event-tile-text`, `--event-tile-session-bg`, `--event-tile-assessment-bg`, `--event-tile-checkin-bg`, `--event-tile-group-bg`, `--event-tile-blocked-bg`, `--event-tile-completed-bg`, `--event-tile-completed-text`.
- Updated `getEventColor()` in `src/pages/Schedule.tsx` to return CSS custom properties instead of Tailwind `bg-*` classes; fixed the type mapping to `'session'` (the actual `CalendarEvent` type) instead of the non-existent `'1-on-1'`.
- Updated `EventCard` to apply background/text colors via inline styles using the theme tokens.
- **Deviation / note:** `Schedule.tsx` still hardcodes a dark page background (`bg-slate-950 text-white`), so the light-theme schedule screenshot is visually dark. The tile tokens are theme-ready, but a full Schedule light-mode refactor is out of scope for this phase.

### Item 4 — Playwright tile-click modal assertion
- Fixed pre-existing click bug: `EventCard` now has a direct `onClick` that opens `SessionDetailDialog`; previously, cell-level click used the cell hour as the time, so sessions starting at :30 could not be opened.
- Added temporary smoke spec `.temp/audit/smoke-64.spec.ts` and serial config `.temp/audit/playwright.config.ts`.
- Smoke coverage:
  1. Direct `/schedule` deep route returns HTTP 200 and the app shell.
  2. Week grid renders the known UTC-boundary session on its local day; screenshots at 390px/834px in both themes; computed-style assertion that tile background ≠ `rgb(100, 116, 139)`; zero horizontal overflow.
  3. Clicking a session tile opens `SessionDetailDialog` with visible title/time/status and Edit/Delete buttons.

### Gate results
- `npx tsc -b` ✅
- `npm run lint` ✅
- `npm run test` ✅ (468 tests, +3 new)
- `npm run build` ✅ (`dist/404.html` identical to `dist/index.html`)
- `npm run test:e2e` ✅ (4/4 existing smoke tests)
- Phase 64 temp smoke ✅ (3/3 tests)

## Phase 65A — Program Creator: Step 5 split sync + Step 6 exercise bugs + change-exercise UX

**Branch:** `feat/phase-65a-program-creator-fixes` off `main` (`aba6830`)  
**Goal:** Fix the owner-reported Step 5 Weekly Split sync bug (split type doesn't update the weekly plan/summary) and Step 6 Exercise Review bugs (exercises repeat across days; wrong movement patterns e.g. Barbell Row on a Push day); ship the Similar / Swap-from-day / Custom change-exercise UX using the 52B muscle taxonomy for pattern classification.

### Root causes (verified in code before touching anything)
- `applySplit` only re-seeded `workoutExercises` for the Full Body branch; every other split-type change replaced day labels but left the old split's lists in place (the literal Barbell-Row-on-Push-day repro was a stale Full-Body rotation). `applySplit` also dropped every workout-row `dbId` → next save deleted + re-inserted all workout rows.
- `toggleDay` flipped `active` only; list-less days silently duplicated Day 1's list via the shared-`exercises` fallback (and editing one row made the copy permanent).
- Generator: `pickExercise`'s reuse fallback could repeat a name within one day; the top-up's stage 2 padded short days from the whole pool, pattern-blind.
- The 52B taxonomy (`exercise_library.primary_muscle/secondary_muscle`) was public-read and unused by the wizard.

### What shipped (no schema/type/migration changes, no new deps)
- `src/lib/splitSeeder.ts` — deterministic pattern-aware day seeding from the hardcoded category pools + `reconcileWorkoutExercises` (keys ⊆ active days; unchanged day+label carries list; else reseed) + `preserveSplitIds` (workout-row identity). 'Full Body X' labels keep the curated Task-3 rotation.
- `src/lib/exerciseTaxonomy.ts` — 29-name live muscle vocabulary → push/pull/legs/core/any patterns; 52A normalization + equipment-stripped fallback matching; `similarExercises` ranking with the honest day-fit fallback (unknown name OR day-incompatible current).
- `src/lib/wizardSplit.ts` — `swapExerciseAcrossDays` (whole-row cross-day swap).
- `src/lib/aiProgramGenerator.ts` — tiered `pickExercise` (no same-day repeats) + top-up cycles only the day's own slot categories (pattern-safe; MIN preserved).
- `src/hooks/useExerciseTaxonomy.ts` + `src/components/exercise/ExerciseChangeDialog.tsx` — module-cached public-read taxonomy; Similar / Swap from day / Custom (Custom = existing 31B picker).
- `src/pages/AIProgramBuilder.tsx` — `applySplit`/`toggleDay` reconcile; derived `splitType`; `defaultData` seeded per-day; pattern-aware Auto-Fill; amber pattern chips on flagged rows; "Swap via library…" row button → "Change exercise…".
- NOT touched (documented): Step 7 `program` snapshot card (frozen preview), `handleAutoGenerate`/`mapGeneratedToProgramData`, `exerciseSwap.ts` (other consumers), `clientContext.availability`, `fullBodyRotation.ts`.

### Gate results
- `npx tsc -b` ✅
- `npm run lint` ✅
- `npm run test` ✅ (**495 tests**, +27 new: 12 taxonomy, 10 seeder, 2 cross-day swap, 3 generator)
- `npm run build` ✅
- `npm run test:e2e` ✅ (4/4 existing smoke tests)
- Phase 65A temp smoke ✅ (3/3, deleted after): Full-Body→PPL repro shows no Barbell Row on the Push day + distinct days + derived 'Custom' dropdown + preview match; toggle-off prunes, toggle-on reseeds; Similar applies a suggestion (prescription kept), Swap-from-day exchanges rows both ways, Custom opens the 31B picker, typed Barbell Row gets the "Pull on Push day" chip whose click-through offers day-fitting replacements. Screenshots in `.temp/audit/shots/65a/` (7 shots). Smoke note: the wizard's AnimatePresence row transitions keep exit-ghost rows in the DOM ~300ms — assertions must wait for row-count settle after a swap.
- No DB test data written (UI-only smoke; the auto-generator persists to localStorage only) → no SQL cleanup required.

**Goal:** 5 targeted mobile fixes from a real-browser audit (chat FAB overlap, view-toggle naming, Clients dead gap, misplaced header buttons, bottom-nav crowding), verified responsively in both themes.

- **Item 2 (toggle rename) — DONE.** `src/components/ModeToggle.tsx` — 'Cards / Table' (documented choice); behavior identical.
- **Item 3 (Clients dead gap) — DONE.** `src/pages/Clients.tsx` content wrapper `pt-20` → `pt-4` (Layout's `main` already clears the fixed navbar with `pt-14`; the extra 80px was the dead space).
- **Item 4 (misplaced Edit/Archive) — DONE.** Removed from the Clients list header; both actions remain in the row Actions menu + desktop inline buttons. 'Add New Client' / 'Invite link' kept.
- **Item 5 (bottom-nav crowding) — DONE.** `src/components/Layout.tsx` — 4 primary tabs + More tab opening a z-[70] bottom sheet (Form Checks / Messages / Settings); routes, icons, active-state preserved exactly; ≥768px sidebar untouched.

### Gate results
- `npx tsc -b` ✅ · `npm run lint` ✅ · `npm run test` ✅ (**557 tests** — no pure logic extracted this phase) · `npm run build` ✅ (404 fallback ✅) · repo e2e ✅ 4/4

Screenshots in `.temp/audit/shots/70/`.
- DB: no fixtures used this phase — nothing to clean.
- **Smoke-mechanics lesson (documented for future phases):** computing derived state (`moreActive`) before a `useCallback` it references causes a TDZ crash (`Cannot access 'o' before initialization`) that blanks the whole app — the smoke caught it pre-merge; place derived constants after the callbacks they read. `getBoundingClientRect()` via evaluate is more reliable than `boundingBox()` for fixed/animated elements.
